## Canada Occupational Health and Safety Regulations {#canada-occupational-health-and-safety-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/86-304
:::

[CANADA LABOUR CODE](/eng/acts/L-2)

Registration 1986-03-13

Canada Occupational Health and Safety Regulations

P.C. 1986-616 1986-03-13
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}

the Minister of Labour, pursuant to sections 82[[Footnote

subsection 106(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_1e){#fn_1e-ID0EDWAA} of the [Canada Labour
Code](/eng/acts/L-2), is pleased hereby to revoke

  [[Return to footnote

## [Part I]{.HTitleText1} {#h-893070 .Part}

**[[1.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.1 .sectionLabel}** \[Repealed,
SOR/2002-208, s. 2\]

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893072 .Subheading}

**[[1.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.2 .sectionLabel}** In these Regulations,

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in Parts I to XIX, Part II of the [Canada Labour
      Code](/eng/acts/L-2), and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in Part XX, the [Canada Labour
      Code](/eng/acts/L-2); ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ANSI]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ANSI]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the American National Standards
    Institute; ([ANSI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[approved organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[approved organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an organization
    that is approved by any province for the teaching of first aid;
    ([organisme agréé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[basic first aid certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[basic first aid certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    certificate issued by either a qualified person or the organization
    that developed the training, as the case may be, for successful
    completion of a one-day first aid course; ([certificat de secourisme
    élémentaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[change room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[change room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a room that is used by
    employees to change from their street clothes to their work clothes
    and from their work clothes to their street clothes, and includes a
    locker room; ([vestiaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[CSA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[CSA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian Standards
    Association; ([CSA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[dangerous substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dangerous substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/88-68, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[elevating device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[elevating device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an escalator,
    elevator or other device for moving passengers or freight;
    ([appareil élévateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fire hazard area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fire hazard area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an area that contains
    or is likely to contain explosive or flammable concentrations of
    hazardous substances; ([endroit présentant un risque
    d'incendie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[first aid room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[first aid room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a room that complies
    with the requirements of section 16.10; ([salle de premiers
    soins]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[high voltage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[high voltage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a voltage of 751 volts or
    more between any two conductors or between a conductor and ground;
    ([haute tension]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[locked out]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[locked out]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of any
    equipment, machine or device, that the equipment, machine or device
    has been rendered inoperative and cannot be operated or energized
    without the consent of the person who rendered it inoperative;
    ([verrouillé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[lower explosive limit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[lower explosive limit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the lower limit
    of flammability of a chemical agent or a combination of chemical
    agents at ambient temperature and pressure, expressed

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a gas or vapour, as a percentage in air by
      volume, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for dust, as the weight of dust per volume of
      air; ([limite explosive inférieure]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[medical practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[medical practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/88-68, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2021-118, s.
    5\]]{.Repealed}

[[National Building Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[National Building Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the National
    Building Code of Canada, 1995, issued by the Canadian Commission on
    Building and Fire Codes, National Research Council of Canada, dated
    1995, as amended from time to time; ([Code canadien du
    bâtiment]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[National Fire Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[National Fire Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the National Fire
    Code of Canada 1995, issued by the Canadian Commission on Building
    and Fire Codes, National Research Council of Canada, dated 1995, as
    amended from time to time; ([Code national de prévention des
    incendies du Canada]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[oxygen deficient atmosphere]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[oxygen deficient atmosphere]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
    atmosphere in which there is less than 19.5 per cent by volume of
    oxygen at a pressure of one atmosphere or in which the partial
    pressure of oxygen is less than 148 mm Hg; ([air à faible teneur en
    oxygène]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[personal service room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[personal service room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a change room,
    toilet room, shower room, lunch room, living space, sleeping
    quarters or a combination thereof; ([local réservé aux soins
    personnels]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[protection equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[protection equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means safety materials,
    equipment, devices and clothing; ([équipement de
    protection]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[qualified person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[qualified person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a
    specified duty, a person who, because of his knowledge, training and
    experience, is qualified to perform that duty safely and properly;
    ([personne qualifiée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[readily available]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[readily available]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a
    document, present and easily accessible at the work place at all
    times; ([facilement accessible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[regional office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[regional office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a work place,
    means the office of the Department of Employment and Social
    Development that is responsible for the Department's Labour Program
    in any of the Department's administrative regions in which the work
    place is situated; ([bureau régional]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[toilet room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[toilet room]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a room that contains a
    toilet or a urinal, but does not include an outdoor privy. ([lieux
    d'aisances]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

### [Prescription]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893101 .Subheading}

**[[1.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.3 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations are
prescribed for the purposes of sections 125, 125.1, 125.2, 126 and 146.5
of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 2
- SOR/94-263, s. 4
- [SOR/2023-65, s. 4](#1395117-1395582){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1395117-1395582.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-1.3-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 1.3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893105 .Subheading}

**[[1.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.31 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations
apply to any person who is not an employee but who performs for an
employer to which these Regulations apply activities whose primary
purpose is to enable the person to acquire knowledge or experience, and
to the employer, as if that person were an employee of the employer, and
every provision of these Regulations must be read accordingly.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-211, s. 1
:::

**[[1.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.4 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations do
not apply in respect of employees employed

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} on trains while in operation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} on aircraft while in operation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} on ships;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} subject to Part II of the [Oil and Gas Occupational
  Safety and Health Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-87-612), on or in
  connection with exploration or drilling for or the production,
  conservation, processing or transportation of oil or gas in frontier
  lands, as that term is defined in the [Canada Petroleum Resources
  Act](/eng/acts/C-8.5); or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} on or in connection with a work or undertaking that
  is excluded from the application of the Act by an order made pursuant
  to section 123.1 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/87-623, s. 1
- SOR/94-263, s. 5
- SOR/2009-147, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-1.4-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 1.4"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893117 .Subheading}

**[[1.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.5 .sectionLabel}** If an employer is
required by section 125 or 125.1 of the Act to keep and maintain a
record, the employer shall keep and maintain the record and make it
readily available for examination by the Head of Compliance and
Enforcement and by the policy committee or, if there is no policy
committee, the work place committee or the health and safety
representative for the work place to which it applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 3
- SOR/94-263, s. 6
- SOR/2002-208, s. 3
- SOR/2014-148, s. 2

  ajax-after="1177315-1187816.html"}

Version](section-1.5-20190625.html "Link to previous version of section 1.5"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Inconsistent Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893121 .Subheading}

**[[1.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.6 .sectionLabel}** In the event of an
inconsistency between any standard incorporated by reference in these
Regulations and any other provision of these Regulations, that other
provision shall prevail to the extent of the inconsistency.

**[[1.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.7 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2019-243, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-1.7-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 1.7"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Alternate Media]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893124 .Subheading}

- **[[1.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section,

  [[alternate media]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[alternate media]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any method of
      communication that permits an employee with a special need to
      receive any information, instruction or training required by these
      Regulations to be provided, including braille, large print, audio
      tape, computer disc, sign language and verbal communications;
      ([média substitut]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[highly visible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[highly visible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means marked with brightly
      coloured paint, painted with a reflective coating or marked by
      other means so as to be readily apparent; ([très
      visible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[special need]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[special need]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a need that stems from
      a condition that impairs an employee's ability to receive any
      information, instruction or training that is required by these
      Regulations to be provided. ([besoins spéciaux]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), where an employer or other
  person is required by these Regulations to give, provide or make
  available any information, instruction or training to an employee and
  the employee has a special need, the employer or other person shall
  give, provide or make available the information, instruction or
  training to the employee by means of an alternate medium.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where information, including warnings, is required by
  these Regulations to be provided by means of a sign or marking, the
  alternate medium shall be visible or audible to an employee with a
  special need.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a warning is required to be given by a means
  other than a sign or marking, the warning shall be given to an
  employee with a special need in a manner that effectively warns the
  employee of the nature of the danger.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where an employer or other person is required by
  these Regulations to give, provide or make available any information
  by means of labels, defect tags or lockout tags, the employer or other
  person need not provide the information by means of an alternate
  medium on the labels, defect tags or lockout tags.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-525, s. 1
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 3](#1177317-1187842){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177317-1187842.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-1.8-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 1.8"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Permanent Structures]{.HTitleText1} {#h-893136 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893137 .Subheading}

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Part.

[[ASHRAE]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ASHRAE]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the American Society of
    Heating, Refrigerating and Air-Conditioning Engineers.
    ([ASHRAE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[building]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[building]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a structure that is used or
    intended for supporting or sheltering any use or occupancy, and
    includes a grain-handling facility, a tower, an antenna and an
    antenna-supporting structure. ([bâtiment]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[floor hole]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[floor hole]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an opening in a floor or
    platform that measures less than 300 mm but more than 50 mm in its
    smallest dimension. ([trou dans le plancher]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[floor opening]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[floor opening]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an opening in a floor,
    platform, pavement or yard that measures 300 mm or more in its
    smallest dimension. ([ouverture dans le plancher]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[grain-handling facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[grain-handling facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a structure
    that is constructed, installed or established to handle, store or
    process grain or grain products, and includes an elevator as defined
    in section 2 of the [Canada Grain Act](/eng/acts/G-10).
    ([installation de manutention des grains]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[HVAC system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[HVAC system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heating, ventilating and
    air conditioning system that is installed in a building, and
    includes all of its equipment and components. ([système
    CVCA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[wall opening]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[wall opening]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an opening in a wall or
    partition that measures at least 750 mm in height and 300 mm in
    width. ([ouverture dans un mur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-263, s. 7
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
:::

### [DIVISION I]{.HLabel2}[Buildings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893148 .Subheading}

#### [Standards]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893149 .Subheading}

- **[[2.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  design and construction of every building, the construction of which
  begins on or after the day of the coming into force of this
  subsection, shall meet the requirements of the National Building Code.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every building, the construction of which begins
  before the day of the coming into force of this subsection, shall, if
  feasible, meet the requirements of the National Building Code.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The renovation of any building or part of a building
  shall, if feasible, meet the requirements of the National Building
  Code.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If it is not feasible for an employer to comply with
  the requirements of subsection (3), the employer shall, before the
  proposed renovations start, notify the work place committee or the
  health and safety representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 2(F)
- SOR/96-525, s. 2
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 4
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 4](#1177324-1187865){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177324-1187865.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.2-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2.2"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Doors]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893157 .Subheading}

- **[[2.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every double-action swinging door
  that is located in an exit, entrance or passageway used for two-way
  pedestrian traffic or traffic involving wheelchairs or other similar
  devices shall be designed and fitted in a manner that will allow
  persons who are approaching from one side of the door to be aware of
  persons who are on the other side of it.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The area of every passageway into which a door or
  gate extends when open, other than the door of a closet or other small
  unoccupied storage room, shall be marked, in consultation with the
  work place committee or the health and safety representative in a
  manner that clearly indicates the area of hazard created by the
  opening of the door or gate.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a door or gate that is to remain open extends
  into a passageway for a distance that will reduce the effective width
  of the passageway to a width less than that required by the National
  Building Code,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an attendant shall be posted near the open door or
    gate; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a highly visible barricade shall be placed across
    the passageway before the door or gate is opened to prevent persons
    from using the passageway while the door or gate is open.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 3(F)
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 5
:::

#### [Clearances]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893166 .Subheading}

**[[2.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.4 .sectionLabel}** A window awning or
canopy or any part of a building that projects over an exterior
passageway shall be installed or constructed in a manner that allows a
clearance of not less than 2.2 m between the passageway surface and the
lowest projection of the awning or canopy or projecting part of the
building.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-525, s. 3
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
:::

#### [Floor and Wall Openings]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893170 .Subheading}

- **[[2.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an employee has access to a
  wall opening from which there is a drop of more than 1.2 m or to a
  floor opening, highly visible guardrails shall be fitted around the
  wall opening or floor opening or it shall be covered with material
  capable of supporting all loads that may be brought to bear on it.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The material shall be securely fastened to and
  supported by structural members.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to vehicle maintenance
  pits or to the loading and unloading areas of truck, railroad or
  marine docks the edges of which are marked in a highly visible manner.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a pit is used for the maintenance of vehicles,
  including railway rolling stock, the hazard it represents shall be
  clearly identified and its perimeter shall be delineated by highly
  visible markings.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 4(F)
- SOR/96-525, s. 4
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
:::

#### [Open-top Bins, Hoppers, Vats and Pits]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893178 .Subheading}

- **[[2.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an employee has access to an
  open-top bin, hopper, vat, pit or other open-top enclosure from a
  point directly above the enclosure, the enclosure shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} covered with a grating, screen or other covering
    that will prevent the employee from falling into the enclosure; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provided with a walkway that is not less than 500
    mm wide and is fitted with highly visible guardrails.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The grating, screen, covering or walkway shall be
  designed, constructed and maintained so that it will support a load
  that is not less than the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the maximum load that may be brought to bear on it,
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a live load of 6 kPa.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where an employee is working above an open-top bin,
  hopper, vat, pit or other open-top enclosure that is not covered with
  a grating, screen or other covering, the inside wall of the enclosure
  shall be fitted with a fixed ladder, except where the operations
  carried on in the enclosure make such a fitting impracticable.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every enclosure referred to in subsection (1) whose
  walls extend less than 1.1 m above an adjacent floor or platform used
  by employees shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} covered with a grating, screen or other covering;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fitted with a highly visible guardrail; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} guarded by a person in order to prevent employees
    from falling into the enclosure.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 5(F)](#1177326-1187879){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177326-1187879.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.6-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2.6"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Ladders, Stairways and Ramps]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893193 .Subheading}

**[[2.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.7 .sectionLabel}** Where an employee is
required to move from one level to another that is more than 450 mm
higher or lower than the first level, the employer shall install a fixed
ladder, stairway or ramp between the levels.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-525, s. 5
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
:::

**[[2.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.8 .sectionLabel}** Where one end of a
stairway is so close to a traffic route used by vehicles, to a machine
or to any other hazard as to be hazardous to the safety of an employee
using the stairway, the employer shall, at that end of the stairway,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} post a sign to warn employees of the hazard; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if feasible, install a highly visible barricade that
  will protect employees using the stairway from the hazard.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-525, s. 6
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 6(E)](#1177328-1187880){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177328-1187880.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.8-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2.8"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[2.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fixed ladder installed after the day of the coming into force of this
  section shall be designed, constructed and installed in accordance
  with the requirements of ANSI Standard A14.3-1984 entitled American
  National Standard for Ladders --- Fixed --- Safety Requirements, as
  amended from time to time, other than section 7 of that Standard.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A fixed ladder that is installed before the day of
  the coming into force of this section shall, if feasible, meet the
  requirements referred to in subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} While ascending or descending a fixed ladder, an
  employee shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} maintain a three-point contact with the ladder; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} carry any tools, equipment or materials in a pouch
    or holster or in any other secure manner.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A fixed ladder shall be highly visible or its
  presence otherwise brought to the attention of any employee in the
  area.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- SOR/2011-206, s. 1
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 7](#1177330-1187887){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177330-1187887.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.9-20110930.html "Link to previous version of section 2.9"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[2.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.10
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every grain-handling facility that
  has a manlift or ladder that provides access to a floor or roof over a
  grain bin or silo shall, in addition to its primary exit, have a
  secondary exit from the floor or roof that consists of an exterior
  fixed ladder constructed in accordance with the requirements referred
  to in subsection 2.9(1).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A fixed ladder that is installed in a grain-handling
  facility before the day of the coming into force of this section
  shall, if feasible, meet the requirements referred to in subsection
  2.9(1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A fixed ladder that is installed adjacent to a
  manlift and that is intended for emergency exit from the manlift shall
  meet the requirements of section 5.1.9 of CSA Standard B 311-M1979,
  entitled Safety Code for Manlifts, the English version of which is
  dated October 1979 and the French version of which is dated July 1984.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-525, s. 7
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 8](#1177332-1187926){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177332-1187926.html"}
- [SOR/2022-94, s. 5(F)](#1343801-1344383){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343801-1344383.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.10-20190625.html "Link to previous version of section 2.10"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Docks, Ramps and Dock Plates]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893217 .Subheading}

- **[[2.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every loading and unloading dock and
  ramp shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} of sufficient strength to support the maximum load
    that may be brought to bear on it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} free of surface irregularities that may interfere
    with the safe operation of mobile equipment; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} fitted around its sides that are not used for
    loading or unloading with side rails, curbs or rolled edges of
    sufficient height and strength to prevent mobile equipment from
    running over the edge.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Cross-traffic on dock levellers shall be limited to
  the portion of the dock leveller where there is no danger of tipping
  the materials handling equipment.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every portable ramp and every dock plate shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} clearly marked or tagged to indicate the maximum
    safe load that it is capable of supporting; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} installed so that it cannot slide, move or
    otherwise be displaced under the load that may be brought to bear on
    it.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
:::

#### [Guardrails]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893229 .Subheading}

- **[[2.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every guardrail shall be highly
  visible and consist of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a horizontal top rail not less than 900 mm but not
    more than 1 100 mm above the base of the guardrail;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a horizontal intermediate rail spaced midway
    between the top rail and the base; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} supporting posts spaced not more than 3 m apart at
    their centres.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every guardrail shall be designed to withstand a
  static load of 890 N applied in any direction at any point on the top
  rail.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-263, s. 8(F)
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
:::

#### [Toe Boards]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893238 .Subheading}

**[[2.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.13 .sectionLabel}** If there is a risk
that tools or other objects could fall onto a person from a platform or
other raised area, or through a floor opening or floor hole,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a toe board that extends from the floor of the
  platform or other raised area to a height of not less than 125 mm
  shall be installed; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the tools or other objects are piled to such a
  height that a toe board would not prevent the tools or other objects
  from falling, a solid or mesh panel shall be installed from the floor
  to a height of not less than 450 mm.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 9](#1177334-1187929){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177334-1187929.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.13-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2.13"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Housekeeping and Maintenance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893244 .Subheading}

- **[[2.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every exterior stairway, walkway,
  ramp and passageway that may be used by employees shall be kept free
  of accumulations of ice and snow or other slipping or tripping
  hazards.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} All dust, dirt, waste and scrap material in every
  work place in a building shall be removed as often as is necessary to
  protect the health and safety of employees and shall be disposed of in
  such a manner that the health and safety of employees is not
  endangered.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every travelled surface in a work place shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} slip resistant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} kept free of splinters, holes, loose boards and
    tiles and similar defects.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 6
:::

**[[2.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.15 .sectionLabel}** Where a floor in a
work place is normally wet and employees in the work place do not use
non-slip waterproof footwear, the floor shall be covered with a dry
false floor or platform or treated with a non-slip material or
substance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 5(F)
- SOR/96-525, s. 8
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
:::

- **[[2.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.16
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The cleaning of a window on any
  level above the ground floor level of a building the construction of
  which begins on or after the day of the coming into force of this
  subsection shall be done in accordance with the requirements of CSA
  Standard CAN/CSA-Z91-M90, entitled Safety Code for Window Cleaning
  Operations, as amended from time to time.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} To the extent reasonably practicable, the cleaning of
  a window on any level above the ground floor level of a building the
  construction of which began before the day of the coming into force of
  this subsection shall be done in accordance with the requirements
  referred to in subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (4) to (8) apply to buildings whose
  owners are employers as defined in subsection 122(1) of the Act.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall, in respect of buildings the
  construction of which begins on or after the day of the coming into
  force of this subsection, ensure that a qualified person inspects the
  anchor points and permanently installed suspended platforms used for
  window cleaning and that they meet the requirements referred to in
  subsection (1).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall, in respect of buildings the
  construction of which began before the day of the coming into force of
  this subsection, ensure that a qualified person inspects the anchor
  points and permanently installed suspended platforms used for window
  cleaning and that they meet the requirements of CSA Standard
  Z91-M1980, entitled Safety Code for Window Cleaning Operations, the
  English version of which is dated May 1980 and the French version of
  which is dated November 1983.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The inspection of anchor points and permanently
  installed suspended platforms shall be done

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} before they are used for the first time;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as often as necessary, but at least as often as
    recommended by their manufacturer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} whenever they are reported as being defective; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in every case, at least once a year.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Immediately on completion of an inspection, the
  qualified person shall, in a written, signed and dated report, inform
  the employer of any defects or hazardous conditions detected in the
  anchor points or permanently installed suspended platforms.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that any reported defect in the anchor
    points or permanently installed suspended platforms is repaired
    before they are used;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that any maintenance and repairs are
    performed in accordance with the manufacturer's recommendations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} keep, for a period of two years, a record of any
    inspection and maintenance, including the date the inspection or
    maintenance was carried out and the name of the person who did it;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} keep, for as long as the anchor points and
    permanently installed suspended platforms are used, a record of the
    modifications or repairs made to them, including the day the work
    was done and the name of the person who did it.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- [SOR/2022-94, s. 5(F)](#1343801-1344384){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343801-1344384.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.16-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2.16"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Temporary Heating]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893275 .Subheading}

- **[[2.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), where a
  high-capacity portable open-flame heating device is used in an
  enclosed work place, the heating device shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be located, protected and used so that there is no
    hazard of igniting tarpaulins, wood or other combustible materials
    near the heating device;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be used only when there is ventilation provided;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be located so as to be protected from accidental
    contact, damage or overturning; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} not restrict a means of exit.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the heating device does not provide complete
  combustion of the fuel used in connection with it, it shall be
  equipped with an exhaust system that discharges the products of
  combustion outside the enclosed work place.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
:::

### [DIVISION II]{.HLabel2}[Towers, Antennas and Antenna-Supporting Structures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893285 .Subheading}

- **[[2.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  employee shall climb a tower, an antenna or an antenna-supporting
  structure unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the employer has authorized the employee to do so;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employee has been trained and instructed in a
    safe method of climbing; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the employer has provided a personal
    fall-protection system in accordance with Part XII.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No employee shall climb or work on a tower, an
  antenna or an antenna-supporting structure

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when weather conditions are likely to be hazardous
    to the health or safety of the employee, except when the work is
    required to remove a hazard or to rescue an employee; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the physical condition of the tower, antenna or
    antenna-supporting structure is likely to be hazardous to the health
    or safety of the employee.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 41
- [SOR/2019-243, s. 2](#1175883-1187502){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1175883-1187502.html"}
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 10(F)](#1177336-1187944){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177336-1187944.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.18-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2.18"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[2.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.19 .sectionLabel}** Where reasonably
practicable, the design and construction of every tower, antenna and
antenna-supporting structure the construction of which begins on or
after the day of the coming into force of this section shall meet the
requirements of CSA Standard CAN/CSA-S37-94, entitled Antennas, Towers,
and Antenna-Supporting Structures, as amended from time to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- [SOR/2022-94, s. 5(F)](#1343801-1344385){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343801-1344385.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.19-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2.19"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION III]{.HLabel2}[HVAC Systems]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893299 .Subheading}

#### [Application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893300 .Subheading}

**[[2.20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.20 .sectionLabel}** Sections 2.21 to 2.24
apply to every work place that is equipped with an HVAC system and that
is under the employer's control.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- SOR/2011-206, s. 2
- SOR/2014-142, s. 1(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.20-20110930.html "Link to previous version of section 2.20"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Standards]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893304 .Subheading}

**[[2.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.21 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section
2.22, every HVAC system installed on or after the day of the coming into
force of this section shall meet the design requirements of ASHRAE
Standard 62-1989, entitled Ventilation for Acceptable Indoor Air
Quality, as amended from time to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
:::

**[[2.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.22 .sectionLabel}** If feasible, the
relevant portion of an HVAC system shall be modified to meet the design
requirements referred to in section 2.21 if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the level of occupancy in a building or portion of a
  building exceeds the level for which the HVAC system was designed; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the use of a building or portion of a building
  differs from the use for which the HVAC system was designed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 11](#1177338-1187946){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177338-1187946.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.22-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2.22"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Records]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893313 .Subheading}

- **[[2.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For HVAC systems installed on or
  after the day of the coming into force of this section, the employer
  shall keep a record of the information required by section A-2.3.5.2
  of Appendix A of the National Building Code and make the record
  readily available.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the record required by subsection (1),
  the employer shall keep and make readily available a record of the
  normal hours of occupancy and the types of activities of the occupants
  of the building.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of HVAC systems to which subsection (1)
  does not apply, the employer shall, if feasible, keep and make readily
  available all records required by subsections (1) and (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 12](#1177340-1187957){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177340-1187957.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.23-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2.23"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Operation, Inspection, Testing, Cleaning and Maintenance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893320 .Subheading}

- **[[2.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.24
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall appoint a
  qualified person to set out, in writing, instructions for the
  operation, inspection, testing, cleaning and maintenance of an HVAC
  system and the calibration of probes or sensors on which the system
  relies. In writing the instructions, the qualified person shall take
  into account CSA Guideline Z204-94, entitled Guideline for Managing
  Air Quality in Office Buildings, dated June 1994.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The instructions shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2011-206, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be readily available;

  - [(c) and (d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2009-147, s.
    3\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} specify the manner of operation of the HVAC system;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} specify the nature and frequency of inspections,
    testing, cleaning and maintenance; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} be reviewed by a qualified person and, if
    necessary, amended

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} when modifications to the HVAC system are carried
      out in accordance with section 2.22,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} when the standard referred to in section 2.21 is
      amended,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} when the an investigation carried out in
      accordance with section 2.27 has identified that a health or
      safety hazard exists, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} at least every five years.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (2)(c), if an investigation
  referred to in section 2.27 identifies that a health or safety hazard
  exists, the instructions shall be developed and made readily available
  without delay.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall appoint a qualified person or
  persons to implement the instructions and make a report, in writing,
  of each inspection, testing, cleaning and maintenance operation.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The report shall be kept readily available by the
  employer for a period of at least five years and shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specify the date and type of work performed, and
    the identity of the person who performed it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} identify the components of the HVAC system or
    portion of an HVAC system involved; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} record test results, any deficiencies observed and
    the actions taken to correct them.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2

**[[2.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.25 .sectionLabel}** An employer shall
ensure that the qualified person or persons referred to in subsection
2.24(4) are instructed and trained in the procedures to be followed in
the operation, inspection, testing, cleaning and maintenance of the HVAC
system and the calibration of probes or sensors on which the system
relies.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- SOR/2009-147, s. 4(F)
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 13](#1177342-1187959){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177342-1187959.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.25-20090528.html "Link to previous version of section 2.25"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[2.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.26 .sectionLabel}** An employer shall
post, in a place readily accessible to every employee, the telephone
number of a contact person to whom health or safety concerns regarding
the indoor air quality in the work place can be directed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 42
:::

#### [Investigations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-893348 .Subheading}

- **[[2.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall develop, or
  appoint a qualified person to develop, a procedure for investigating
  situations in which the health or safety of an employee in the work
  place is or may be endangered by the air quality.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The procedure shall include the following steps:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a review of the nature and number of health or
    safety complaints;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a visual inspection of the work place;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the inspection of the HVAC system for cleanliness,
    operation and performance;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a review of the maintenance schedule for the HVAC
    system;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the assessment of building use as compared to the
    use for which it was designed;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the assessment of actual level of occupancy as
    compared to the level for which the building was designed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the determination of potential sources of
    contaminants;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the determination of levels of carbon dioxide,
    carbon monoxide, temperature, humidity and air motion, where
    necessary;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the specification, where necessary, of tests to be
    conducted to determine levels of formaldehyde, particulates,
    airborne fungi and volatile organic compounds; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the identification of the standards or guidelines
    to be used in evaluating test results.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In developing the procedure, the employer or the
  qualified person appointed by the employer shall take into account the
  Department of Health publication 93-EHD-166, entitled Indoor Air
  Quality in Office Buildings: A Technical Guide.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the health or safety of an employee in a work
  place is or may be endangered by the air quality, the employer shall,
  without delay, appoint a qualified person to carry out an
  investigation in accordance with the procedure developed pursuant to
  subsection (1).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The investigation shall be carried out in
  consultation with the work place committee or the health and safety
  representative.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} If feasible, the employer shall, in consultation with
  the work place committee or the health and safety representative,
  remove or control any health or safety hazard that is identified in
  the course of the investigation.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall keep the records of every indoor
  air quality complaint and investigation for at least five years.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, ss. 7, 40, 42
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 14](#1177344-1187962){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177344-1187962.html"}
:::

:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Temporary Structures and Excavations]{.HTitleText1} {#h-893369 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893370 .Subheading}

**[[3.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.1 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies to
portable ladders, temporary ramps and stairs, temporary elevated work
bases used by employees and temporary elevated platforms used for
materials.

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893372 .Subheading}

**[[3.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.2 .sectionLabel}** No employee shall use a
temporary structure if it is feasible to use a permanent structure.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-263, s. 9(F)
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 15](#1177346-1187973){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177346-1187973.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3.2-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 3.2"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[3.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.3 .sectionLabel}** No employee shall work
on a temporary structure in rain, snow, hail or an electrical or wind
storm that is likely to be hazardous to the health or safety of the
employee, except if the work is required to remove a hazard or to rescue
an employee.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-208, s. 8
:::

**[[3.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.4 .sectionLabel}** Tools, equipment and
materials used on a temporary structure shall be arranged or secured in
such a manner that they cannot be knocked off the structure
accidentally.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 6(F)
:::

**[[3.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.5 .sectionLabel}** No employee shall use a
temporary structure unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} he has authority from his employer to use it; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} he has been trained and instructed in its safe and
  proper use.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 16(F)](#1177348-1187977){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177348-1187977.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3.5-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 3.5"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[3.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Prior to a work shift, a qualified
  person shall make a visual safety inspection of every temporary
  structure to be used during that shift.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an inspection made in accordance with
  subsection (1) reveals a defect or condition that adversely affects
  the structural integrity of a temporary structure, no employee shall
  use the temporary structure until the defect or condition is remedied.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-208, s. 9(F)
:::

### [Barricades]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893390 .Subheading}

**[[3.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.7 .sectionLabel}** Where a person or a
vehicle may come into contact with a temporary structure, a person shall
be positioned at the base of the temporary structure or a highly visible
barricade shall be installed around it to prevent any such contact.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-525, s. 9
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 17(F)](#1177350-1187978){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177350-1187978.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3.7-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 3.7"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Guardrails and Toe Boards]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893394 .Subheading}

- **[[3.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Guardrails and toe boards shall be
  installed at every open edge of a platform of a temporary structure.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The guardrails and toe boards referred to in
  subsection (1) shall meet the standards set out in sections 2.12 and
  2.13.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-374, s. 3
:::

### [Temporary Stairs, Ramps and Platforms]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893400 .Subheading}

- **[[3.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 3.10(3),
  temporary stairs, ramps and platforms shall be designed, constructed
  and maintained to support any load that is likely to be imposed on
  them and to allow safe passage of persons and equipment on them.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Temporary stairs shall have

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} uniform steps in the same flight;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a slope not exceeding 1.2 in 1; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a hand-rail that is not less than 900 mm and not
    more than 1 100 mm above the stair level on open sides including
    landings.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Temporary ramps and platforms shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} securely fastened in place;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} braced if necessary to ensure their stability; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} provided with cleats or surfaced in a manner that
    provides a safe footing for employees.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A temporary ramp shall be so constructed that its
  slope does not exceed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the temporary ramp is installed in the
    stairwell of a building not exceeding two storeys in height, 1 in 1,
    if cross cleats are provided at regular intervals not exceeding 300
    mm; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, 1 in 3.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 18(F)](#1177352-1187980){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177352-1187980.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3.9-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 3.9"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Scaffolds]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893414 .Subheading}

- **[[3.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.10
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The erection, use, dismantling or
  removal of a scaffold shall be carried out by or under the supervision
  of a qualified person.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The footings and supports of every scaffold shall be
  capable of carrying, without dangerous settling, all loads that are
  likely to be imposed on them.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every scaffold shall be capable of supporting at
  least four times the load that is likely to be imposed on it.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The platform of every scaffold shall be at least 480
  mm wide and securely fastened in place.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 19(F)](#1177354-1187989){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177354-1187989.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3.10-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 3.10"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Portable Ladders]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893420 .Subheading}

- **[[3.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Commercially manufactured portable
  ladders shall meet the standards set out in CSA Standard CAN3-Z11-M81,
  Portable Ladders, the English version of which is dated September,
  1981, as amended to March, 1983 and the French version of which is
  dated August, 1982, as amended to June, 1983.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), every portable ladder
  shall, while being used,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be placed on a firm footing; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be secured in such a manner that it cannot be
    dislodged accidentally from its position.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where, because of the nature of the location or of
  the work being done, a portable ladder cannot be securely fastened in
  place, it shall, while being used, be sloped so that the base of the
  ladder is not less than one-quarter and not more than one-third of the
  length of the ladder from a point directly below the top of the ladder
  and at the same level as the base.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every portable ladder that provides access from one
  level to another shall extend at least three rungs above the higher
  level.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Metal or wire-bound portable ladders shall not be
  used if there is a risk that they could come into contact with any
  live electrical circuit or equipment.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} No employee shall work from any of the three top
  rungs of any single or extension portable ladder or from either of the
  two top steps of any portable step ladder.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 7(F)
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 20(E)](#1177356-1187991){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177356-1187991.html"}
- [SOR/2022-94, s. 5(F)](#1343801-1344387){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343801-1344387.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3.11-20190625.html "Link to previous version of section 3.11"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Excavation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893432 .Subheading}

- **[[3.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before the commencement of work on a
  tunnel, excavation or trench, the employer shall mark the location of
  all underground pipes, cables and conduits in the area where the work
  is to be done.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an excavation or trench constitutes a hazard to
  employees, a highly visible barricade shall be installed around it.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In a tunnel or in an excavation or trench that is
  more than 1.4 m deep and whose sides are sloped at an angle of 45° or
  more to the horizontal

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the walls of the tunnel, excavation or trench, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the roof of the tunnel

  shall be supported by shoring and bracing that is installed as the
  tunnel, excavation or trench is being excavated.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply in respect of a trench
  where the employer provides a system of shoring composed of steel
  plates and bracing, welded or bolted together, that can support the
  walls of the trench from the ground level to the trench bottom and can
  be moved along as work progresses.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The installation and removal of the shoring and
  bracing referred to in subsection (3) shall be performed or supervised
  by a qualified person.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Tools, machinery, timber, excavated materials or
  other objects shall not be placed within 1 m from the edge of an
  excavation or trench.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-525, s. 10
:::

### [Safety Nets]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893445 .Subheading}

- **[[3.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  there is a risk that tools, equipment or materials could fall onto or
  from a temporary structure, the employer shall provide a protective
  structure or a safety net to protect from injury any employee on or
  below the temporary structure.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The design, construction and installation of a safety
  net referred to in subsection (1) shall meet the standards set out in
  ANSI Standard ANSI A10.11-1979, American National Standard for Safety
  Nets Used During Construction, Repair and Demolition Operations, dated
  August 7, 1979.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 21(E)](#1177358-1187998){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177358-1187998.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3.13-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 3.13"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Housekeeping]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893449 .Subheading}

**[[3.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.14 .sectionLabel}** Every platform,
hand-rail, guardrail and work area on a temporary structure used by an
employee shall be kept free of accumulations of ice and snow while the
temporary structure is in use.

**[[3.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.15 .sectionLabel}** The floor of a
temporary structure used by an employee shall be kept free of grease,
oil or other slippery substance and of any material or object that may
cause an employee to trip.

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Elevating Devices]{.HTitleText1} {#h-893452 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893453 .Subheading}

**[[4.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.1 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not
apply to elevating devices used in the underground workings of mines.

### [Standards]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893455 .Subheading}

- **[[4.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every elevating device and every
  safety device attached thereto shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} meet the standards set out in the applicable CSA
    standard referred to in subsection (2) if feasible; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be used, operated and maintained in accordance with
    the standards set out in the applicable CSA standard referred to in
    subsection (2).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the applicable
  CSA standard for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} elevators, dumbwaiters, escalators and moving walks
    is CSA Standard CAN3-B44-M85, Safety Code for Elevators, other than
    clause 9.1.4 thereof, the English version of which is dated November
    1985 and the French version of which is dated March 1986;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} manlifts is CSA Standard B311-M1979, Safety Code
    for Manlifts, the English version of which is dated October, 1979
    and the French version of which is dated July, 1984 and Supplement
    No. 1-1984 to B311-M1979, the English version of which is dated
    June, 1984 and the French version of which is dated August, 1984;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} elevating devices for the handicapped is CSA
    Standard CAN3-B355-M81, Safety Code for Elevating Devices for the
    Handicapped, the English version of which is dated April, 1981 and
    the French version of which is dated December, 1981.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 8
- SOR/94-263, s. 10
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 22](#1177360-1188000){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177360-1188000.html"}
- [SOR/2022-94, s. 5(F)](#1343801-1344388){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343801-1344388.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4.2-20190625.html "Link to previous version of section 4.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Use and Operation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893466 .Subheading}

**[[4.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.3 .sectionLabel}** No elevating device
shall be used or operated with a load in excess of the load that it was
designed and installed to move safely.

- **[[4.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), no
  elevating device shall be used or operated while any safety device
  attached to it is inoperative.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), no safety device attached
  to an elevating device shall be altered, interfered with or rendered
  inoperative.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to an elevating
  device or a safety device that is being inspected, tested, repaired or
  maintained by a qualified person.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-122, s. 1(E)](#1299263-1300230){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1299263-1300230.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4.4-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 4.4"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Inspection and Testing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893472 .Subheading}

**[[4.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.5 .sectionLabel}** Every elevating device
and every safety device attached thereto shall be inspected and tested
by a qualified person to determine that the prescribed standards are met

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} before the elevating device and the safety device
  attached to it are operated;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} after an alteration to the elevating device or a
  safety device attached thereto; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} once every 12 months.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-122, s. 2(E)](#1299265-1300232){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1299265-1300232.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4.5-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 4.5"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[4.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  record of each inspection and test made in accordance with section 4.5
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be signed by the person who made the inspection and
    test;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} include the date of the inspection and test and the
    identification and location of the elevating device and safety
    device that were inspected and tested; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} set out the observations of the person inspecting
    and testing the elevating device and safety device on the safety of
    the devices.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every record referred to in subsection (1) shall be
  made by the employer and kept by him in the work place in which the
  elevating device is located for a period of two years after the date
  on which it is signed in accordance with paragraph (1)(a).

### [Repair and Maintenance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893483 .Subheading}

**[[4.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.7 .sectionLabel}** Repair and maintenance
of elevating devices or safety devices attached thereto shall be
performed by a qualified person appointed by the employer.

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Boilers and Pressure Vessels]{.HTitleText1} {#h-893485 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-893486 .Subheading}

**[[5.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.1 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Part.

[[Boiler Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Boiler Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means CSA Standard B51-97,
    Boiler, Pressure Vessel, and Pressure Piping Code, the English
    version of which is dated September 1997 and was amended in February
    1998 and the French version of which is dated September 1998. ([Code
    des chaudières]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[high pressure boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[high pressure boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a boiler in which
    steam, gas or vapour is generated at more than one atmosphere of
    pressure or a boiler containing liquid that has a working pressure
    exceeding 1,100 kPa or an operating temperature exceeding 121°C.
    ([chaudièreà haute pression]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person recognized under
    the laws of any province or by the National Board of Boiler and
    Pressure Vessel Inspectors as qualified to inspect boilers, pressure
    vessels and pressure piping systems. ([inspecteur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[low pressure hot water boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[low pressure hot water boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hot
    water boiler that has a pressure not exceeding 1,100 kPa and an
    operating temperature not exceeding 121°C. ([chaudière à eau chaude
    basse pression]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[low pressure organic fluid boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[low pressure organic fluid boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    boiler that contains an organic fluid, that has an operating
    temperature not exceeding 343°C and that has no valves or other
    obstructions to prevent circulation of the organic fluid between the
    boiler and an expansion tank that is fully vented to the atmosphere.
    ([chaudière à basse pression contenant un
    hydrocarbure]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[low pressure steam boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[low pressure steam boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a boiler
    that operates at a steam pressure not exceeding one atmosphere of
    pressure. ([chaudière à vapeur d'eau basse
    pression]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[low working pressure hot water boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[low working pressure hot water
    boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2009-147, s.
    5\]]{.Repealed}

[[maximum allowable working pressure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[maximum allowable working pressure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    maximum working pressure that is specified by the manufacturer in
    the plans and specifications accepted and registered by the
    provincial authorities and that is set out in the record referred to
    in section 5.18. ([pression de fonctionnement maximale
    autorisée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[maximum temperature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[maximum temperature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the maximum
    temperature that is specified by the manufacturer in the plans and
    specifications accepted and registered by the provincial authorities
    and that is set out in the record referred to in section 5.18.
    ([température maximale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[NDT technician]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[NDT technician]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who performs
    nondestructive testing and who is certified to carry out such
    testing. ([technicien END]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pipeline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pipeline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section
    2 of the [Canadian Energy Regulator Act](/eng/acts/C-15.1), but does
    not include boilers. ([pipeline]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pressure piping system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pressure piping system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an assembly of
    pipes, pipe fittings, valves, safety devices, pumps, compressors and
    other fixed equipment that contains a gas, vapour or liquid and is
    connected to a boiler or pressure vessel. ([réseau de canalisations
    sous pression]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[unfired boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[unfired boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a boiler in which steam
    or other vapour is generated by a source of heat other than
    combustion and includes an electric boiler. ([chaudière sans
    combustion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[waste heat boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[waste heat boiler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an indirect-fired
    boiler that utilizes waste gases produced by the primary process to
    generate steam. ([chaudière à récupération de
    chaleur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 9
- SOR/2001-284, s. 1
- SOR/2009-147, s. 5(E)
- SOR/2014-142, s. 2

  ajax-after="1299267-1300235.html"}
