### [DIVISION II]{.HLabel2}[Hazardous Substances Other than Hazardous Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-894584 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2002-208, s. 43(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 20

\]
:::

#### [Asbestos Exposure Management Program]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894587 .Subheading}

##### [Asbestos-containing Material]{.HTitleText4} {#h-894588 .Subheading}

- **[[10.26.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If asbestos-containing material is
  present in a work place and there is the potential for a release of
  asbestos fibres or employee exposure to asbestos fibres, an employer
  shall ensure that the qualified person who is carrying out a hazard
  investigation under section 10.4 takes into consideration the type of
  asbestos, the condition of the asbestos-containing material, the
  friability of the asbestos-containing material, the accessibility to
  and likelihood of damage to the asbestos-containing material and the
  potential for the release of asbestos fibres and employee exposure to
  asbestos fibres.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} At the completion of an investigation carried out
  under section 10.4, the employer shall ensure that a record of the
  location, friability and condition of the asbestos-containing material
  and the type of asbestos contained in that material is kept and made
  readily available for examination by employees and is in any form as
  determined in consultation with the policy committee or, if there is
  no policy committee, the work place committee or the health and safety
  representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 67](#1177503-1188109){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177503-1188109.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.26.1-20170620.html "Link to previous version of section 10.26.1"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Asbestos Exposure Control Plan]{.HTitleText4} {#h-894594 .Subheading}

**[[10.26.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.2 .sectionLabel}** Before
undertaking any work activity that involves asbestos-containing
material, an employer shall, in consultation with the policy committee
or, if there is no policy committee, the work place committee or the
health and safety representative, develop, implement and administer an
asbestos exposure control plan that requires the employer to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that a hazard investigation under section 10.4
  has been carried out by a qualified person and, in the event that
  there is a change in the work activity, review any report that was
  prepared as a result of the investigation and, if necessary, have a
  qualified person carry out another investigation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that a qualified person classifies the work
  activity as a low-risk activity, moderate-risk activity or high-risk
  activity;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all asbestos-containing material present
  in the work place that is exposed or that will be disturbed is
  identified by signs and labels or by any other effective manner;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all friable asbestos-containing material
  present in the work place is controlled by removal, enclosure or
  encapsulation or by any other effective manner to prevent employee
  exposure to asbestos;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} ensure that procedures and control measures for
  moderate-risk activities and high-risk activities are developed and
  implemented; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} develop and implement an employee education and
  training program that is specific to asbestos-containing material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
:::

**[[10.26.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.3 .sectionLabel}** If an employee
who is undertaking automotive service procedures may be exposed to
asbestos from friction material or dust arising from that material, an
employer shall ensure that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the use of compressed air, brushes or similar means
  to dry-remove friction material dust from automotive assemblies is
  prohibited; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} signs to advise employees of the hazards and required
  precautions are posted in service work areas where friction material
  is handled or dust arising from that material is generated.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
:::

##### [Asbestos Dust, Waste and Debris Removal]{.HTitleText4} {#h-894609 .Subheading}

- **[[10.26.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} During any work activities that
  involve friable asbestos-containing materials, an employer shall
  ensure that the following activities are carried out frequently and at
  regular intervals as determined by a qualified person, at the end of
  each work shift and immediately after the work activity is completed:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all asbestos dust, waste and debris are removed by
    vacuuming with a vacuum cleaner that is equipped with a HEPA filter,
    damp-mopping or wet-sweeping the area that is contaminated with the
    asbestos dust, waste or debris; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any drop sheets that are contaminated with asbestos
    dust, waste or debris are wetted.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} All asbestos dust, waste or debris and any drop
  sheets that are contaminated with asbestos dust, waste or debris shall
  be placed in a container referred to in section 10.26.11.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
:::

**[[10.26.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.5 .sectionLabel}** If a glove bag
is used for the removal of asbestos insulation from pipes, ducts and
similar structures, an employer shall ensure that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the glove bag is sealed to prevent the release of
  asbestos fibres into the work area;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the glove bag is inspected for damage or defects
  immediately before it is attached to the pipe, duct or similar
  structure and at regular intervals during its use;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} all waste from asbestos-containing material that is
  on surfaces is washed to the bottom of the glove bag and all exposed
  asbestos-containing material is encapsulated when it is inside the
  glove bag;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the glove bag is evacuated using a vacuum cleaner
  that is equipped with a HEPA filter to remove the air inside the bag
  prior to the removal of the glove bag; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} after the glove bag is removed, all exposed surfaces
  are cleaned with a damp cloth and a vacuum cleaner that is equipped
  with a HEPA filter.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
:::

##### [Decontamination]{.HTitleText4} {#h-894625 .Subheading}

- **[[10.26.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before leaving a work area that is
  contaminated with asbestos-containing material, an employee shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if their protective clothing is to be reused,
    decontaminate the clothing with a damp cloth or a vacuum cleaner
    that is equipped with a HEPA filter before taking the clothing off;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if their protective clothing is not to be reused,
    place the clothing in a container referred to in section 10.26.11.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An employer shall provide employees with a facility
  reserved for washing their hands and face, and employees shall wash
  their hands and face using that facility before leaving a work area
  that is contaminated with asbestos-containing material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
:::

**[[10.26.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.7 .sectionLabel}** As soon as
feasible after any work activity that involves asbestos-containing
material is completed, an employee shall clean reusable tools,
equipment, rigid barriers and portable enclosures that are contaminated
with asbestos with a damp cloth or a vacuum cleaner that is equipped
with a HEPA filter.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 68(E)](#1177505-1188111){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177505-1188111.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.26.7-20170620.html "Link to previous version of section 10.26.7"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Air Sampling]{.HTitleText4} {#h-894636 .Subheading}

- **[[10.26.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An employer shall ensure that a
  qualified person takes air samples to test for airborne asbestos
  fibres

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the vicinity of the containment system during
    any work activity that involves asbestos-containing material and, in
    the case of a work activity that lasts longer than 24 hours, at
    least daily;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the clean room during removal and clean-up
    operations and, in the case of removal and clean-up operations that
    last longer than 24 hours, at least daily; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in contaminated areas that are inside the
    containment system as necessary during removal and clean-up
    operations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall ensure that the following air
  samples are taken:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} two samples for every area in an enclosure that is
    10 m^2^ or less;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} three samples for every area in an enclosure that
    is more than 10 m^2^ and not more than 500 m^2^; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} five samples for every area in an enclosure that is
    more than 500 m^2^.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Within 24 hours after obtaining the air sampling test
  results, the employer shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} post a copy of the results in a conspicuous place
    in the work place; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make the results available to the policy committee,
    if any, the work place committee and the health and safety
    representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
:::

##### [Clearance Air Sampling]{.HTitleText4} {#h-894651 .Subheading}

- **[[10.26.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before dismantling a containment
  system and after all asbestos dust, waste and debris have been cleaned
  up, removed or encapsulated, an employer shall ensure that clearance
  air samples are taken inside the enclosure and that the concentration
  of airborne asbestos fibres is determined in accordance with Method
  7400 set out in the document entitled NIOSH Manual of Analytical
  Methods, published by the National Institute for Occupational Safety
  and Health, as amended from time to time, or in accordance with a
  scientifically proven method used to collect and analyze a
  representative sample of airborne asbestos fibres.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} When conducting clearance air sampling, the employer
  shall ensure that forced air is used inside the enclosure to dislodge
  any asbestos fibres from all surfaces and keep them airborne.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Clearance air sampling shall be taken until the
  concentrations of airborne asbestos fibres do not exceed the values
  referred to in subsection 10.19(1.1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
:::

**[[10.26.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.10 .sectionLabel}** Within 24
hours after obtaining the clearance air sampling test results, the
employer shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} post a copy of the results in a conspicuous place in
  the work place; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} make the results available to the policy committee,
  if any, the work place committee and the health and safety
  representative, and provide a copy of the results to the Head of
  Compliance and Enforcement.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
- [SOR/2021-118, s. 6](#1295135-1312225){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1295135-1312225.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.26.10-20170620.html "Link to previous version of section 10.26.10"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Containers for Asbestos Dust, Waste and Debris]{.HTitleText4} {#h-894663 .Subheading}

**[[10.26.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.26.11 .sectionLabel}** Containers
for the containment of asbestos dust, waste and debris and
asbestos-containing material shall be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} dust-tight;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} suitable to contain asbestos dust, waste or debris;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} impervious to asbestos;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} identified as containing asbestos dust, waste or
  debris;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} cleaned with a damp cloth or a vacuum cleaner that is
  equipped with a HEPA filter immediately before being removed from the
  work area; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} removed from the work place frequently and at regular
  intervals as determined by a qualified person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-132, s. 4
:::

#### [Identification]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894673 .Subheading}

**[[10.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.27 .sectionLabel}** Every container of
a hazardous substance, other than a hazardous product, that is stored,
handled, used or disposed of in the work place shall be labelled in a
manner that discloses clearly

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the generic name of the substance; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the hazard information in respect of the substance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 43(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 20
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.27-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.27"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[10.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.28 .sectionLabel}** If a safety data
sheet in respect of a hazardous substance, other than a hazardous
product, that is stored, handled or used in the work place may be
obtained from the supplier of the hazardous substance, the employer
shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} obtain a copy of the safety data sheet; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} keep the copy of the safety data sheet readily
  available for examination by employees in any form, as determined in
  consultation with the policy committee or, if there is no policy
  committee, the work place committee or the health and safety
  representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 43(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 9
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 69(F)](#1177507-1188113){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177507-1188113.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-10.28-20160614.html "Link to previous version of section 10.28"){.PITLink}

### [DIVISION III]{.HLabel2}[Hazardous Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-894684 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2016-141, s. 20

\]
:::

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894687 .Subheading}

**[[10.29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.29 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,

[[bulk shipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bulk shipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 5.5(1) of the [Hazardous Products
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17); ([expédition en
    vrac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any package or receptacle,
    including a bag, barrel, bottle, box, can, cylinder, drum and
    storage tank; ([contenant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fugitive emission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fugitive emission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hazardous product
    in gas, liquid, solid, vapour, fume, mist, fog or dust form that
    escapes from processing equipment, from control emission equipment
    or from a product into the work place; ([émission
    fugitive]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hazardous waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hazardous waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hazardous product
    that is acquired or generated for recycling or recovery or is
    intended for disposal; ([résidu dangereux]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[laboratory sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[laboratory sample]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 5(1) of the [Hazardous Products
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17); ([échantillon pour
    laboratoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[manufactured article]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[manufactured article]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2016-141, s. 10\]]{.Repealed}

[[research and development]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[research and development]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a
    hazardous product, means systematic investigation or search carried
    out in a field of science or technology by means of experiment or
    analysis, other than investigation or search in respect of market
    research, sales promotion, quality control or routine testing of
    hazardous products, and includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} applied research, namely, work undertaken for the
      advancement of scientific knowledge with a specific practical
      application in view, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} development, namely, use of the results of
      applied research for the purpose of creating new, or improving
      existing, processes or hazardous products; ([recherche et
      développement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[risk phrase]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[risk phrase]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a hazardous
    product, means a statement identifying a hazard that may arise from
    the exposure to the hazardous product; ([mention de
    risque]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes offer for sale, expose for
    sale and distribute; ([vente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[significant new data]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[significant new data]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in subsection 5.12(1) of the [Hazardous Products
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17); ([nouvelles données
    importantes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[supplier label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[supplier label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a label prepared by a
    supplier that discloses any information elements required by the
    [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3); ([étiquette du
    fournisseur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[supplier material safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[supplier material safety data
    sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2016-141, s.
    10\]]{.Repealed}

[[supplier safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[supplier safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a safety
    data sheet prepared by a supplier that discloses any information
    elements required by the [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3);
    ([fiche de données de sécurité du fournisseur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[work place label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work place label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a label prepared by
    an employer in accordance with this Division; ([étiquette du lieu de
    travail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[work place material safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work place material safety data
    sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2016-141, s.
    10\]]{.Repealed}

[[work place safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[work place safety data sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a safety
    data sheet prepared by an employer in accordance with subsection
    10.33(1) or (2). ([fiche de données de sécurité du lieu de
    travail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 43(F)
- SOR/2016-141, ss. 10, 20
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.29-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.29"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894709 .Subheading}

- **[[10.30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.30
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This Division does not apply in
  respect of any

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} tobacco or a tobacco product as defined in section
    2 of the [Tobacco and Vaping Products Act](/eng/acts/T-11.5);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} manufactured article as defined in section 2 of the
    [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} wood or a product made of wood.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Division, other than section 10.43, does not
  apply in respect of hazardous waste.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2016-141, s. 11
- 2018, c. 9, s. 77
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.30-20160614.html "Link to previous version of section 10.30"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Safety Data Sheets and Labels in Respect of Certain Hazardous Products]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894718 .Subheading}

- **[[10.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.31
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  section 10.42, every employer shall implement the provisions of
  sections 10.27 and 10.28 in respect of a hazardous product and may, in
  so doing, replace the generic name of the product with the brand name,
  chemical name, common name or trade name, if the hazardous product

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is present in the work place;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} was received from a supplier; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is one of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a hazardous product, other than wood or a product
      made of wood, that is listed in Schedule 1 to the [Hazardous
      Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a nuclear substance, as defined in section 2 of
      the [Nuclear Safety and Control Act](/eng/acts/N-28.3), that is
      radioactive.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An employer may store a hazardous product received
  from a supplier without having a supplier label on it, without having
  obtained a safety data sheet for it and without having conducted an
  employee education and training program with respect to the matters
  referred to in subparagraphs 10.14(2)(a)(ii) and (c)(ii)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} while the employer is actively seeking a supplier
    label and a supplier safety data sheet for the hazardous product;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if labelling affixed to the container of the
    hazardous product containing information on the hazardous product is
    not removed, defaced, modified or altered.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/94-263, s. 38
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 20
- SOR/2016-141, s. 12
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.31-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.31"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Supplier Safety Data Sheets]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894731 .Subheading}

- **[[10.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a hazardous product, other than a
  hazardous product referred to in paragraph 10.31(1)(c), is received in
  the work place by an employer, the employer shall, without delay,
  obtain a supplier safety data sheet in respect of the hazardous
  product from the supplier, unless the employer is already in
  possession of a supplier safety data sheet that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is for a hazardous product that both has the same
    product identifier and is from the same supplier;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} discloses information that is current at the time
    that the hazardous product is received; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} was prepared and dated less than three years before
    the day on which the hazardous product is received.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the supplier safety data sheet in respect of a
  hazardous product in a work place is three years old or more, the
  employer shall, if feasible, obtain from the supplier a current
  supplier safety data sheet.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If it is not feasible for an employer to obtain a
  current supplier safety data sheet, the employer shall update the
  hazard information on the most recent supplier safety data sheet that
  the employer has received, on the basis of the ingredients disclosed
  on that supplier safety data sheet and on the basis of any significant
  new data that becomes available to the employer.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The employer is exempt from the requirements of
  subsection (1) if a laboratory sample of a hazardous product is
  received in the work place from a supplier who is exempted by the
  [Hazardous Products Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17) from
  the requirement to provide a safety data sheet for that product.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/94-263, s. 39
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/98-427, s. 7
- SOR/2016-141, s. 12
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 70](#1177509-1188114){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177509-1188114.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.32-20160614.html "Link to previous version of section 10.32"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Work Place Safety Data Sheets]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894742 .Subheading}

- **[[10.33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.33
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 10.42, if an
  employer produces in the work place a hazardous product, other than a
  fugitive emission or an intermediate product undergoing reaction
  within a reaction or process vessel, or imports into Canada a
  hazardous product and brings it into the work place, the employer
  shall prepare a work place safety data sheet in respect of that
  hazardous product.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 10.42, if an employer receives a
  supplier safety data sheet, the employer may prepare a work place
  safety data sheet to be used in the work place in place of the
  supplier safety data sheet if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the work place safety data sheet discloses at least
    the information disclosed on the supplier safety data sheet;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information disclosed on the work place safety
    data sheet does not disclaim or contradict the information disclosed
    on the supplier safety data sheet;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the supplier safety data sheet is readily available
    for examination by employees in any form, as determined in
    consultation with the policy committee or, if there is no policy
    committee, the work place committee or the health and safety
    representative; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the work place safety data sheet discloses that the
    supplier safety data sheet is available in the work place.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An employer shall review the accuracy of the
  information disclosed on a work place safety data sheet referred to in
  subsection (1) or (2) and update it as soon as feasible after new
  hazard information or significant new data becomes available to the
  employer.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the information required to be disclosed on the
  work place safety data sheet is not available or not applicable to the
  hazardous product, the employer shall, in place of the information,
  insert the words "not available" or "not applicable", as the case may
  be, in the English version and the words "[non disponible]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}" or "[sans objet]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", as the case may
  be, in the French version, of the work place safety data sheet.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68. s. 12
- SOR/94-263, s. 40
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2016-141, s. 12
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 71(E)](#1177514-1188117){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177514-1188117.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.33-20160614.html "Link to previous version of section 10.33"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Availability of Safety Data Sheets]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894754 .Subheading}

- **[[10.34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.34
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall, in any work
  place in which an employee is likely to handle or be exposed to a
  hazardous product, keep readily available a copy of the work place
  safety data sheet or the supplier safety data sheet, as the case may
  be, in English and in French, for examination by employees and by any
  policy committee, work place committee or health and safety
  representative.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The work place safety data sheet and supplier safety
  data sheet shall be made available in any form, as determined in
  consultation with the policy committee or, if there is no policy
  committee, the work place committee or the health and safety
  representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 21
- SOR/2016-141, s. 12
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 72](#1177516-1188119){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177516-1188119.html"}
:::

Version](section-10.34-20160614.html "Link to previous version of section 10.34"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Labels]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894762 .Subheading}

- **[[10.35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.35
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 10.37 to 10.39,
  each hazardous product, other than a hazardous product referred to in
  paragraph 10.31(1)(c), in a work place and each container in which the
  hazardous product is contained in a work place shall, if the hazardous
  product or the container is received from a supplier,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the hazardous product is in a
    bulk shipment, be accompanied by a supplier label;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the employer has undertaken in
    writing to apply a label to the inner container of the hazardous
    product, have applied to it a supplier label as soon as feasible
    after the hazardous product is received from the supplier; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, have applied to it a supplier
    label.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 10.37 to 10.39 and 10.42, if a
  hazardous product, other than a hazardous product referred to in
  paragraph 10.31(1)(c), is received from a supplier and an employer
  places the hazardous product in the work place in a container other
  than the container in which it was received from the supplier, the
  employer shall apply to the container a supplier label or work place
  label that discloses the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the product identifier;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the hazard information in respect of the hazardous
    product; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that a safety data sheet for
    the hazardous product is available in the work place.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 10.41 and 10.42, no person shall
  remove, deface, modify or alter the supplier label applied to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a hazardous product that is in the work place; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the container of a hazardous product that is in the
    work place.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/94-263, s. 41
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2016-141, s. 12
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 73(E)](#1177518-1188122){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177518-1188122.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.35-20160614.html "Link to previous version of section 10.35"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[10.36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.36
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 10.37 to 10.39,
  if an employer produces in the work place a hazardous product, other
  than a fugitive emission, or imports into Canada a hazardous product
  and brings it into the work place, and the hazardous product is not in
  a container, the employer shall disclose the following information on
  a work place label applied to the hazardous product or on a sign
  posted in a conspicuous place in the work place:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the product identifier;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the hazard information in respect of the hazardous
    product; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that a work place safety
    data sheet for the hazardous product is available in the work place.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 10.37 and 10.39, where an
  employer produces a hazardous product in the work place, other than a
  fugitive emission, or imports into Canada and brings into the work
  place a hazardous product, and places the hazardous product in a
  container, the employer shall apply to the container a work place
  label that discloses the information referred to in paragraphs (1)(a)
  to (c).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply in respect of a
  hazardous product that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} intended for export, if the information referred to
    in paragraphs (1)(a) to (c) is disclosed on a sign posted in a
    conspicuous place in the work place; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} packaged in a container and offered for sale in
    Canada, if the container is or is in the process of being
    appropriately labelled for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2016-141, ss. 13, 20
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.36-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.36"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Portable Containers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894788 .Subheading}

**[[10.37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.37 .sectionLabel}** If an employer
stores a hazardous product in the work place in a container that has
applied to it a supplier label or a work place label, a portable
container filled from that container is exempted from the labelling
requirements under section 10.35 or 10.36 if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous product is required for immediate use;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the following conditions apply in respect of the
  hazardous product:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it is under the control of and used exclusively by
    the employee who filled the portable container,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is used only during the work shift in which the
    portable container was filled, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} it is clearly identified by a work place label
    applied to the portable container that discloses the product
    identifier.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 22(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 14
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.37-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.37"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Special Cases]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894797 .Subheading}

**[[10.38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.38 .sectionLabel}** An employer shall,
in a conspicuous place near a hazardous product, post a sign in respect
of the hazardous product that discloses the product identifier if the
hazardous product is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in a process, reaction or storage vessel;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in a continuous-run container;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a bulk shipment that is not placed in a container
  at the work place; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} not in a container and stored in bulk.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2016-141, s. 15
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.38-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.38"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Laboratories]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894805 .Subheading}

- **[[10.39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.39
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a laboratory sample of a
  hazardous product is the subject of a labelling exemption under
  subsection 5(5) of the [Hazardous Products
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17), a label that is provided
  by the supplier and is affixed to, printed on or attached to the
  container of the sample received at the work place and that discloses
  the following information in place of the information required under
  paragraph 3(1)(d) of those Regulations is considered to comply with
  the requirements set out in section 10.35 with respect to a supplier
  label:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if known by the supplier, the chemical name or
    generic chemical name of any material that is in the hazardous
    product and that is classified under the [Hazardous Products
    Act](/eng/acts/H-3) and the [Hazardous Products
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17) as a biohazardous
    infectious material; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the statement "Hazardous Laboratory Sample. For
    hazard information or in an emergency, call/[Échantillon pour
    laboratoire de produit dangereux. Pour obtenir des renseignements
    sur les dangers ou en cas d'urgence, composez]{.otherLang
    lang="fr"}", followed by an emergency telephone number for the
    purpose of obtaining the information that must be provided on the
    safety data sheet of a hazardous product.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a laboratory sample of a hazardous product is the
  subject of a labelling exemption under subsection 5(6) of the
  [Hazardous Products Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17), a
  label that is provided by the supplier and is affixed to, printed on
  or attached to the container of the sample received at the work place
  and that discloses the following information in place of the
  information required under paragraph 3(1)(c) or (d) of those
  Regulations is considered to comply with the requirements set out in
  section 10.35 with respect to a supplier label:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if known by the supplier, the chemical name or
    generic chemical name of any material or substance that is in the
    hazardous product and that is referred to in subsection 3(2) of
    Schedule 1 to the [Hazardous Products
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the statement "Hazardous Laboratory Sample. For
    hazard information or in an emergency, call/[Échantillon pour
    laboratoire de produit dangereux. Pour obtenir des renseignements
    sur les dangers ou en cas d'urgence, composez]{.otherLang
    lang="fr"}", followed by an emergency telephone number for the
    purpose of obtaining the information that must be provided on the
    safety data sheet of a hazardous product.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a hazardous product is in a container other than
  the container in which it was received from a supplier or is produced
  in the work place, the employer is exempt from the requirements set
  out in section 10.36 and subparagraph 10.37(b)(iii) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the employer has complied with subsection (4);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} employee education and training is provided as
    required by these Regulations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous product

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is a laboratory sample,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is intended by the employer to be used solely
      for analysis, testing or evaluation in a laboratory, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is clearly identified through any mode of
      identification visible to employees at the work place.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (3)(a), the employer
  shall ensure that the mode of identification used and the employee
  education and training provided enable the employees to readily
  identify and obtain either the information required on a safety data
  sheet or the information set out in subsections (1) and (2) with
  respect to the hazardous product or laboratory sample.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The employer is exempt from the requirements of
  section 10.35 if a laboratory sample of a hazardous product is
  received in a work place from a supplier who is exempted by the
  [Hazardous Products Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17) from
  the requirement to provide a label for that product.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/98-427, s. 8
- SOR/2016-141, s. 16
:::

#### [Signs]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894824 .Subheading}

**[[10.40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.40 .sectionLabel}** The information
disclosed on a sign referred to in subsection 10.36(1), paragraph
10.36(3)(a), section 10.38 or paragraph 10.43(b) shall be of such a size
that it is clearly legible to employees.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
:::

#### [Replacing Labels]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894828 .Subheading}

- **[[10.41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.41
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, in a work place, a label applied
  to a hazardous product or the container of a hazardous product becomes
  illegible or is removed from the hazardous product or the container,
  the employer shall replace the label with a work place label that
  discloses the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the product identifier;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the hazard information in respect of the hazardous
    product; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that a safety data sheet for
    the hazardous product is available in the work place.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An employer shall review the accuracy of the
  information on a work place label and update it as soon as feasible
  after new hazard information or significant new data becomes available
  to the employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2016-141, s. 17
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 74(E)](#1177520-1188125){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177520-1188125.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.41-20160614.html "Link to previous version of section 10.41"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Exemptions from Disclosure]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894837 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2016-141, s. 18(F)

\]
:::

- **[[10.42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.42
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), if an
  employer has filed a claim for exemption from the requirement to
  disclose information on a safety data sheet or on a label under
  subsection 11(2) of the [Hazardous Materials Information Review
  Act](/eng/acts/H-2.7), the employer shall disclose, in place of the
  information that the employer is exempt from disclosing,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if there is no final disposition of the proceedings
    in relation to the claim, the date on which the claim for exemption
    was filed and the registry number assigned to the claim under
    section 10 of the [Hazardous Materials Information Review
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-88-456); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the final disposition of the proceedings in
    relation to the claim is that the claim is valid, a statement that
    an exemption has been granted and the date on which the exemption
    was granted.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a claim for exemption is in respect of a product
  identifier, the employer shall, on the safety data sheet or label of
  the hazardous product, disclose, in place of that product identifier,
  a code name or code number specified by the employer as the product
  identifier for that hazardous product.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2016-141, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.42-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.42"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Hazardous Waste]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894847 .Subheading}

- **[[10.43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.43
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a hazardous product in the work
  place is hazardous waste, the employer shall disclose the generic name
  and hazard information in respect of the hazardous product by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} applying a label to the hazardous waste or its
    container; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} posting a sign in a conspicuous place near the
    hazardous waste or its container.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall provide education and training to
  employees regarding the safe storage and handling of hazardous waste
  that is found in the work place.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 12
- SOR/94-263, s. 42
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 43(F)
- SOR/2016-141, s. 19
:::

Version](section-10.43-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.43"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Information Required in a Medical Emergency]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894855 .Subheading}

**[[10.44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.44 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 125.2(1) of the Act, a medical professional is a nurse who is
registered or licensed under the laws of a province.

#### [Fire and Explosion Prevention]{.HTitleText3} {#h-894859 .Subheading}

- **[[10.45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.45
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/98-427, s.
  9\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of interpreting the standards
  referred to in sections 10.46 to 10.49,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2000-374, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [[dangerous goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
    [[hazardous products]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} used in these
    Regulations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a hazardous product classified under
    the [Hazardous Products Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} [[flammable liquids]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      [[flammable liquids]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} used in these
      Regulations,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} [[combustible liquids]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
      means [[combustible liquids]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} used in these

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} [[compressed gases]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      [[compressed gases]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} used in these
      Regulations,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} [[reactive substances]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
      means [[reactive flammable materials]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} used in

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} [[aerosol products]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      [[flammable aerosols]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} used in these

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} [[poisonous and infectious
      substances]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means [[poisonous and infectious
      materials]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} used in these Regulations,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} [[corrosive substances]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}\"
      means [[corrosive materials]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} used in these
      Regulations, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} [[oxidizing substances]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[10.46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.46 .sectionLabel}** Hazardous products
shall be stored in accordance with subsections 3.2.7, 3.2.8, 3.2.9 and
3.3.4 of the National Fire Code.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2000-374, s. 5
- SOR/2016-141, s. 20
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10.46-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10.46"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[10.47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.47 .sectionLabel}** Flammable aerosols
shall be stored in accordance with subsection 3.2.5 of the National Fire
Code.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2000-374, s. 5
:::

**[[10.48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.48 .sectionLabel}** The provisions of
Part 3 of the National Fire Code apply as follows:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} compressed gases shall be stored and handled in
  accordance with subsections 3.2.8 and 3.3.5;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} reactive flammable materials shall be stored and
  handled in accordance with subsections 3.2.7 and 3.3.4;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} poisonous and infectious materials shall be stored
  and handled in accordance with subsections 3.2.7, 3.2.8 and 3.3.4;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} corrosive materials shall be stored and handled in
  accordance with subsections 3.2.7, 3.2.8 and 3.3.4; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} oxidizing materials shall be stored and handled in
  accordance with subsections 3.2.7, 3.2.8 and 3.3.4.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2000-374, s. 5
:::

**[[10.49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.49 .sectionLabel}** The provisions of
Part 4 of the National Fire Code apply as follows:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} areas used for the storage, handling and use of
  flammable liquids and combustible liquids shall meet the standards set
  out in subsection 4.1.5, with the exception of article 4.1.5.6;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the drainage and disposal of flammable liquids and
  combustible liquids shall be in accordance with subsection 4.1.6;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} flammable liquids and combustible liquids shall be
  stored in storage tanks meeting the standards set out in subsection
  4.1.8;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} maintenance and operating procedures shall be
  established to prevent the escape of flammable liquids and combustible
  liquids, as required by subsection 4.1.6;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} general container storage and handling of flammable
  liquids and combustible liquids shall be in accordance with
  subsections 4.2.1 to 4.2.8, with the exception of paragraph
  4.2.8.4(d);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} rooms used for container storage of flammable liquids
  and combustible liquids shall conform with subsection 4.2.9, with the
  exception of article 4.2.9.3;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} cabinets used for container storage of flammable
  liquids and combustible liquids shall conform with subsection 4.2.10;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} outdoor container storage of flammable liquids and
  combustible liquids shall be in accordance with subsection 4.2.11;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} storage tanks for flammable liquids and combustible
  liquids shall conform with section 4.3, with the exception of
  paragraph 4.3.13.1(1)(d), articles 4.3.13.5 and 4.3.15.2 and sentences
  4.3.16.1(3) and (4);

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} piping and transfer systems for flammable liquids and
  combustible liquids shall conform with section 4.4, with the exception
  of articles 4.4.6.2, 4.4.11.1 and 4.4.11.2; and

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} flammable liquid and combustible liquid installations
  on piers and wharves shall conform with section 4.7, with the
  exception of article 4.7.10.2.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-294, s. 2
- SOR/2000-374, s. 5
- SOR/2011-206, s. 4(E)
:::

## [PART XI]{.HLabel1}[Confined Spaces]{.HTitleText1} {#h-894904 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-894905 .Subheading}

**[[11.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.01 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[class of confined spaces]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[class of confined spaces]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a group of at
    least two confined spaces that are likely, because of their
    similarity, to present the same hazards to persons entering, exiting
    or occupying them. ([catégorie d'espaces clos]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[confined space]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[confined space]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a space that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is enclosed or partially enclosed;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not designed or intended for continuous human
      occupancy; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has a limited or restricted means of entry or
      exit or an internal configuration that could complicate provision
      of first aid, evacuation, rescue or other emergency
      response. ([espace clos]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hazardous confined space]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hazardous confined space]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a confined
    space that, when entered, occupied or exited by persons, presents
    hazards likely to cause injury, illness or other adverse health
    effects to persons entering, exiting or occupying it because of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its design, construction, location or atmosphere;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the materials or substances in it; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other conditions relating to it. ([espace
      clos dangereux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hot work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hot work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any work where a flame is
    used or a source of ignition may be produced. ([travail à
    chaud]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-143, s. 1](#1303186-1326968){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1303186-1326968.html"}
:::

### [Confined Space Identification]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1326969 .Subheading}

- **[[11.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The employer must, in consultation
  with the work place committee or health and safety representative,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} survey the work place in order to determine whether
    it contains confined spaces that might be entered by persons in
    order to perform work for the employer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} identify each confined space; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} appoint a qualified person to determine whether a
    confined space is a hazardous confined space.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that the qualified person
  provides them with a list of confined spaces including those
  identified as hazardous confined spaces.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that there is a sign or
  marking at the entrance to each confined space indicating that:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is a confined space or a hazardous confined
    space; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it must not be entered without the authorization of
    the employer.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The employer must keep a record of all confined
  spaces referred to in subsection (2) and ensure that it is up-to-date
  and readily accessible to persons before they enter those spaces. The
  employer may either keep the record in the work place or keep a
  centralized record of confined spaces in respect of several work
  places in one work place.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If it is likely that an employee will enter a
  confined space that has been determined not to be a hazardous confined
  space in order to perform work for the employer, the employer must
  establish procedures with respect to safe entry and exit as well as
  person-check and emergency response systems to ensure the continued
  safety of employees.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-143, s. 1](#1303186-1326982){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1303186-1326982.html"}
:::

### [Hazard Assessment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1326983 .Subheading}

- **[[11.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.03
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If it is likely that a person will
  enter a hazardous confined space in order to perform work for an
  employer, the employer must appoint one or more qualified persons to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} carry out an assessment of the hazards to which the
    person is likely to be exposed in that hazardous confined space or
    in a hazardous confined space of the class to which it belongs; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specify the tests that are necessary to determine
    whether the person is likely to be exposed to any of the hazards
    identified under paragraph (a).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that the qualified person or
  persons record the findings of the assessment in a signed and dated
  report to the employer that specifies the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the protection equipment referred to in part XII
    that is to be used by every person granted access to the hazardous
    confined space by the employer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any insulated protection equipment and tools
    referred to in Part VIII that a person may need in the hazardous
    confined space;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the protection equipment and emergency equipment to
    be used by any person who takes part in the rescue of a person from
    the hazardous confined space or who responds to other emergency
    situations in the hazardous confined space;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any requirement for a first aid attendant;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} steps to control or mitigate any hazards; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any situation in which an entry permit system is
    required.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The employer must make a copy of the report available
  to the policy committee, if any, and the work place committee or the
  health and safety representative.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the employer must ensure
  that the report and the list made under subsections (2) and 11.02(2)
  respectively are reviewed by a qualified person at least once every
  three years. However, if there is reason to believe that the
  conditions inside a confined space have changed with respect to the
  last hazard assessment, due to changes to the structure, intended use
  or immediate surrounding area of the space or due to information about
  the space regarding a potential new hazard, the employer must ensure
  that the space is reassessed and treated in accordance with the new
  findings.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a confined space has not been entered in the three
  years preceding the time when the assessment referred to in
  subsection (2) should have been carried out and no entry is scheduled,
  the assessment need not be carried out until it becomes likely that a
  person will enter the confined space in order to perform work for an
  employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-143, s. 1](#1303186-1326999){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1303186-1326999.html"}
:::

### [Confined Space Procedures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1327000 .Subheading}

- **[[11.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.04
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer must --- after
  considering the report made under subsection 11.03(2) in consultation
  with the policy committee, or, if there is no policy committee, the
  work place committee or the health and safety representative ---
  establish procedures, with the date when they are established
  specified in them, that are to be followed by a person entering,
  exiting or occupying a confined space assessed under subsection
  11.03(1).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The procedures apply based on whether the confined
  space is listed as a confined space or a hazardous confined space
  under subsection 11.02(2) and take into account the report made under
  subsection 11.03(2). The procedures include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each confined space

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} safe entry and exit procedures,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} two-way communication and person check systems,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} emergency response measures; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for each hazardous confined space, in addition to
    the requirements specified in paragraph (a), all risk control
    measures that would ensure the health and safety of a person in the
    space and an entry permit system, if required under the report.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an entry permit system is required, the employer
  must ensure that it specifies the length of time that each permit is
  valid, and require that a record be made of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person entering the confined space;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date and time of entry, the anticipated date
    and time of exit and the actual date and time of exit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-143, s. 1](#1303186-1327013){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1303186-1327013.html"}
:::

### [Verification Before Entry in and During Occupancy of Hazardous Confined Space]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1327014 .Subheading}

- **[[11.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.05
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that no
  person is granted access to a hazardous confined space unless the
  employer has first appointed a qualified person to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} verify, by means of tests and continuous
    monitoring, that if the atmosphere inside the hazardous confined
    space could be of concern, compliance with the following
    requirements can be achieved during the period of time that the
    person is in the space:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of any chemical agent or
      combination of chemical agents in the hazardous confined space to
      which the person is likely to be exposed will not exceed

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the value referred to in paragraph 10.19(1)(a)
        for that chemical agent or combination of chemical agents, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the percentage set out in subsection 10.20(1),
        or in subsection 10.20(2) under the circumstances described in
        that subsection for that chemical agent or combination of
        chemical agents,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of any airborne hazardous
      substance, other than a chemical agent, that a person may be
      exposed to in the hazardous confined space is not hazardous to the
      health or safety of the person, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the percentage of oxygen in the air in the
      hazardous confined space is not less than 19.5% by volume and not
      more than 23% by volume, at normal atmospheric pressure;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} verify that the following conditions are met, or if
    that is not feasible, that safety measures have been taken,
    including the use of engineering controls, to ensure the safety of
    the person who will be in the hazardous confined space:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any liquid in which the person could drown has
      been removed from the hazardous confined space,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any free-flowing solid in which the person could
      become entrapped has been removed from the hazardous confined
      space, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous confined space is secured to
      prevent entry of hazardous materials and contaminants; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} verify that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all electrical and mechanical equipment that
      could present a hazard to the person has been disconnected from
      its power source, real or residual, and has been locked out,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the opening for entry into and exit from the
      hazardous confined space is sufficient to allow the safe passage
      of a person using protection equipment, including the protection
      equipment and emergency equipment to be used by a person who takes
      part in a rescue of a person, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 11.06(1), the
      requirements set out in paragraph (a) can be complied with at all
      times when a person is in the hazardous confined space.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that the qualified person
  makes certain that any equipment used to test the atmosphere inside a
  hazardous confined space is used, calibrated and maintained in
  accordance with the manufacturer's instructions.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that the qualified person
  sets out the results of verifications carried out in accordance with
  subsection (1) in a signed and dated report to the employer that
  specifies the test methods applied, the results of the tests and of
  the continuous monitoring, the test equipment used and any failure of
  the test equipment, control devices or systems.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The employer must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the report made under subsection (3) indicates
    that a person who has entered the hazardous confined space was in
    danger, send the report to the policy committee, if any, and to the
    work place committee or the health and safety representative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in all other cases, make the report available, in
    paper or electronic format, to the policy committee or, if there is
    no policy committee, to the work place committee or the health and
    safety representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-143, s. 1](#1303186-1327037){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1303186-1327037.html"}
:::

### [Emergency Procedures and Equipment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1327038 .Subheading}

- **[[11.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.06
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If conditions in a hazardous
  confined space or the nature of the work to be performed in a
  hazardous confined space are such that the requirements set out in
  subsection 11.05(1) cannot be complied with at all times when a person
  is in the hazardous confined space, the employer must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in consultation with the policy committee or, if
    there is no policy committee, the work place committee or the health
    and safety representative, establish emergency procedures to be
    followed in the event of an accident or other emergency in or near
    the hazardous confined space that include

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a plan for reporting the emergency, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a list of necessary resources, including the
      required number of persons, to carry out the emergency procedures;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specify the date when the emergency procedures are
    established and provide a plan for the immediate evacuation of the
    hazardous confined space when

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an alarm is activated, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} there is any significant change in a
      concentration or percentage referred to in paragraph 11.05(1)(a)
      that would adversely affect the health or safety of a person in
      the hazardous confined space;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} provide the protection equipment referred to in
    paragraph 11.03(2)(a) for each employee who is about to enter the
    hazardous confined space;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} ensure that a qualified person trained in the
    emergency procedures established under paragraph (a) and the
    procedures established under section 11.04 is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in attendance outside the hazardous confined
      space, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in communication with the person inside the
      hazardous confined space;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in consultation with the policy committee or, if
    there is no policy committee, the work place committee or the health
    and safety representative, provide the qualified person referred to
    in paragraph (d) with a suitable communication device or alarm for
    summoning assistance; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the required number of persons as
    determined in subparagraph (a)(ii) are available to assist in the
    event of an emergency and to carry out the emergency procedures.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that one of the persons
  referred to in paragraph (1)(f)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is trained in the emergency procedures established
    under paragraph (1)(a) and receives annual refresher training;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has a basic first aid and cardiopulmonary
    resuscitation (CPR) certificate; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is provided with the protection equipment and
    emergency equipment referred to in paragraph 11.03(2)(a).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that every person granted
  access to a hazardous confined space has at their disposal the
  appropriate emergency procedures set out in paragraphs 11.06(1)(a)
  and (b) and that they follow those procedures.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that every person entering,
  exiting or occupying a hazardous confined space referred to in
  subsection (1) wears an appropriate safety harness that is securely
  attached to a lifeline that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is attached to a secure anchor outside the
    hazardous confined space;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is monitored by the qualified person referred to in
    paragraph (1)(d);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} protects the person from the hazard for which it is
    provided and does not in itself create a hazard; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is, if feasible, equipped with a mechanical lifting
    device.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-143, s. 1](#1303186-1327064){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1303186-1327064.html"}
:::

### [Record of Emergency Procedures and Equipment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1327065 .Subheading}

- **[[11.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.07
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a person is about to enter a
  hazardous confined space under circumstances such that the
  requirements set out in paragraph 11.05(1)(a) cannot be complied with,
  the employer must ensure that the qualified person referred to in
  paragraph 11.06(1)(d), in a signed and dated report to the employer,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specifies the emergency procedures established
    under paragraphs 11.06(1)(a) and (b) that are to be followed and the
    protection equipment, insulated protection equipment and tools and
    the emergency equipment that are to be used; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specifies any additional procedures and any other
    equipment that may be needed to ensure the health and safety of the
    person.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that the report made under
  subsection (1) and any emergency procedures specified in it are
  explained by the qualified person to every employee who is about to
  enter a hazardous confined space. Every employee who has received the
  explanation must sign and date a copy of the report to acknowledge
  that they have read it and that it has been explained to them.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-143, s. 1](#1303186-1327072){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1303186-1327072.html"}
:::

### [Provision and Use of Equipment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1327073 .Subheading}

- **[[11.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.08
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The employer must provide

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each employee who is granted access to a hazardous
    confined space with the protection equipment specified under
    paragraph 11.03(2)(a); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each employee who is to take part in rescue
    operations with the protection equipment and emergency equipment
    specified under paragraph 11.03(2)(c).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that every employee who
  enters, exits or occupies a hazardous confined space follows the
  procedures established pursuant to subsection 11.04(1) and uses the
  protection equipment specified under paragraphs 11.03(2)(a) and (b).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any person who is granted access to a hazardous
    confined space has the equipment specified in paragraph 11.03(2)(a),
    failing which the employer must provide it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any person who is to take part in rescue operations
    has the protection equipment and emergency equipment set out in
    paragraph 11.03(2)(c); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any person who enters, exits or occupies a
    hazardous confined space complies with the procedures established in
    subsection 11.04(1) and uses the protection equipment specified in
    paragraphs 11.03(2)(a) and (b).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-143, s. 1](#1303186-1327084){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1303186-1327084.html"}
:::

### [Closing off a Confined Space]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1327085 .Subheading}

**[[11.09]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.09 .sectionLabel}** The employer must
ensure that no person closes off a confined space unless a qualified
person has verified that no person is inside it.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-143, s. 1](#1303186-1327088){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1303186-1327088.html"}
:::

### [Hot Work]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1327089 .Subheading}

- **[[11.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless a qualified person has
  determined that the work can be performed safely, the employer must
  ensure that hot work is not performed in a hazardous confined space
  that contains

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an explosive or flammable hazardous substance in a
    concentration in excess of 10% of its lower explosive limit; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} oxygen in a concentration in excess of 23%.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If hot work is to be performed in a hazardous
  confined space that contains concentrations of flammable or explosive
  materials in excess of the concentrations set out in paragraph (1)(a)
  or (b),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a qualified person must patrol the area surrounding
    the hazardous confined space and maintain a fire-protection watch in
    that area until the fire hazard has passed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employer must ensure that the emergency
    equipment specified under paragraph 11.03(2)(c) is provided in the
    area referred to in paragraph (a).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an airborne hazardous substance can be produced by
  hot work in a hazardous confined space, the employer must ensure that
  no person enters or occupies that hazardous confined space unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the requirements of section 11.11 are met; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that person uses respiratory protection equipment
    that meets the requirements of sections 12.04, 12.05 and 12.13.

::: HistoricalNote

- SOR/92-544, s. 1

### [Ventilation Equipment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895033 .Subheading}

- **[[11.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If ventilation equipment is used to
  maintain the concentration of a chemical agent or combination of
  chemical agents in a hazardous confined space at or below the
  concentration referred to in subparagraph 11.05(1)(a)(i), or to
  maintain the percentage of oxygen in the air of a hazardous confined
  space within the limits referred to in subparagraph 11.05(1)(a)(iii),
  the employer must not grant access to the hazardous confined space
  unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the ventilation equipment is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} equipped with an alarm that will, if the
      equipment fails, be activated automatically and be audible or
      visible to every person in the hazardous confined space, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} monitored by an employee who is in constant
      attendance at the equipment and who is in communication with any
      person in the hazardous confined space; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the ventilation equipment fails, the person will
    have enough time to escape from the hazardous confined space before

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the concentration of the chemical agent or
      combination of chemical agents in the hazardous confined space
      exceeds the concentration referred to in subparagraph
      11.05(1)(a)(i), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the percentage of oxygen in the air of the
      hazardous confined space ceases to remain within the limits
      referred to in subparagraph 11.05(1)(a)(iii).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the ventilation equipment fails, the employee
  referred to in subparagraph (1)(a)(ii) must immediately inform any
  person in the hazardous confined space of the failure of the
  equipment.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the report referred to in subsection 11.03(2)
  determines that a hazardous confined space requires continuous
  ventilation, the employer must ensure continuous atmospheric
  monitoring while the hazardous confined space is occupied.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/92-544, s. 1
- SOR/95-286, s. 10(F)
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 82(F)](#1177544-1188140){.wb-lbx

### [Instruction and Training]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895043 .Subheading}

- **[[11.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The employer must provide every
  employee who is likely to enter a confined space with instruction and
  training in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the procedures and the emergency procedures
    established under subsection 11.04(1) and paragraphs 11.06(1)(a) and
    (b), respectively; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the use of the protection equipment referred to in
    paragraph 11.03(2)(a).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that no person enters a
  confined space unless the person is instructed in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the procedures and the emergency procedures
    established under subsection 11.04(1) and paragraphs 11.06(1)(a) and
    (b), respectively; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the use of the protection equipment referred to in
    paragraph 11.03(2)(a).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The employer must ensure that all persons granted
  access to a confined space have received instruction and training in
  accordance with subsection 11.04(1) and paragraph 11.06(1)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/92-544, s. 1
- [SOR/2021-143, s. 1](#1303186-1327143){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1303186-1327143.html"}
:::
