## [PART XVI]{.HLabel1}[First Aid]{.HTitleText1} {#h-895706 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895707 .Subheading}

**[[16.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.1 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in
this section apply in this Part.

[[ambulance response time]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ambulance response time]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the time
    required for an ambulance with qualified persons and emergency
    medical equipment to reach the work place from the nearest point of
    dispatch under normal travel conditions. ([délai d'intervention
    ambulancière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[first aid attendant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[first aid attendant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a holder of a
    valid basic or standard first aid certificate.
    ([secouriste]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[first aid station]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[first aid station]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place, other than
    a first aid room, at which first aid supplies or equipment are
    stored. ([poste de secours]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[health unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[health unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility that is under
    the charge of a physician or a person who is registered or licensed
    as a nurse under the laws of any province and that, if it is under
    the control of the employer, meets the minimum requirements of a
    first aid room contained in this Part. ([service de
    santé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[medical treatment facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[medical treatment facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hospital,
    medical clinic or physician's office, at which emergency medical
    treatment can be dispensed. ([installation de traitement
    médical]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[remote workplace]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[remote workplace]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a workplace for which
    the ambulance response time is more than two hours. ([lieu de
    travail isolé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[standard first aid certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[standard first aid certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    certificate issued by either a qualified person or the organization
    that developed the training, as the case may be, for successful
    completion of a two-day first aid course; ([certificat de secourisme
    général]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 13(E)
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- SOR/2012-271, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 116](#1177642-1188203){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177642-1188203.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16.1-20121207.html "Link to previous version of section 16.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895718 .Subheading}

- **[[16.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall establish and
  keep up-to-date written instructions that provide for the prompt
  rendering of first aid to an employee for any injury or illness,
  including an occupational illness.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall keep a copy of the instructions
  available at all times for consultation by employees.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 70(F)
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- SOR/2014-142, s. 5
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 117](#1177644-1188206){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177644-1188206.html"}
- [SOR/2025-79, s. 5](#1520560-1524892){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1520560-1524892.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16.2-20190625.html "Link to previous version of section 16.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [First Aid Attendants]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895724 .Subheading}

- **[[16.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At
  every workplace at which six or more employees are working at any
  time, the employer shall ensure that there is a first aid attendant.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} At every remote workplace at which two or more
  employees are working at any time, the employer shall ensure that
  there is a first aid attendant.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} At every workplace at which an employee is working on
  live high voltage electrical equipment, the employer shall ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a first aid attendant is available; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least one of the employees has the training
    necessary to provide resuscitation by mouth-to-mouth resuscitation,
    cardiopulmonary resuscitation or an equivalent direct method.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} At every workplace that is required to have a first
  aid attendant, the employer shall ensure that the first aid attendant
  at the workplace is qualified by having at least

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the workplace is an office workplace for which
    the ambulance response time is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} up to two hours, a basic first aid certificate,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} more than two hours, a standard first aid
      certificate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the workplace is any other workplace, other than
    a workplace in a wilderness area, and the ambulance response time
    for the workplace is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} less than twenty minutes, a basic first aid
      certificate, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} twenty minutes or more but not more than two
      hours, a standard first aid certificate; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the work place is in a wilderness area, a
    standard first aid certificate and a wilderness first aid course
    that is specially designed to meet the first aid needs of persons
    who work, live or travel in such an area.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- SOR/2014-142, s. 6
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 118](#1177649-1188208){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177649-1188208.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16.3-20140529.html "Link to previous version of section 16.3"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[16.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  first aid attendant referred to in section 16.3 or paragraph
  16.10(1)(a)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall be assigned to a first aid station or first
    aid room;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall be available to employees during working
    hours;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shall render first aid to employees who are injured
    or ill at the workplace;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} shall, if required, accompany an injured or ill
    employee to a health unit or a medical treatment facility and render
    first aid in transit;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} shall, in providing care to an injured or ill
    employee, not be overruled by anyone not trained in first aid; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} shall be in charge of providing care for the
    injured or ill employee until the treatment is complete or the
    employee is under the care of an equally or more qualified
    caregiver.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The first aid attendant referred to in subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall work close to the first aid station or first
    aid room to which the first aid attendant is assigned; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall not be assigned duties that will interfere
    with the prompt and adequate rendering of first aid.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- SOR/2014-142, s. 7
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16.4-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 16.4"){.PITLink}
:::

### [First Aid Stations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895754 .Subheading}

- **[[16.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At
  least one first aid station shall be provided for every workplace.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In multi-storey buildings, the location of first aid
  stations shall be such that employees are no more than two stories
  from a first aid station.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every first aid station shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} located at or near the workplace;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} clearly identified by a conspicuous sign; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} accessible during all working hours.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall inspect every first aid station
  regularly, at least monthly, and shall ensure that its contents are
  maintained in a clean, dry and serviceable condition.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if a first aid room,
  health unit or medical treatment facility that meets the requirements
  of subsection (3) is provided by the employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 71(F)
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- SOR/2014-142, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16.5-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 16.5"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Communication of Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895766 .Subheading}

- **[[16.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  employer shall post and keep posted or make available to every
  employee in a conspicuous place in each workplace

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the first aid to be rendered for
    any injury or illness, including an occupational illness;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} information regarding the location of first aid
    stations, first aid rooms, health units and medical treatment
    facilities;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at every first aid station and first aid room, a
    list of first aid attendants, and information on how they may be
    located;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} near the telephones, a list of telephone numbers
    kept up-to-date for use in emergencies; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the transport procedures for
    injured employees.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} At a remote workplace or in a motor vehicle, the
  information and lists referred to in subsection (1) shall be provided
  and retained with the first aid kit.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-525, s. 16
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- SOR/2014-142, s. 9(E)
- [SOR/2021-122, s. 11](#1299285-1300263){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1299285-1300263.html"}
- [SOR/2025-79, s. 6](#1520562-1524894){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1520562-1524894.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16.6-20210604.html "Link to previous version of section 16.6"){.PITLink}
:::

### [First Aid Supplies and Equipment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895777 .Subheading}

- **[[16.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At
  every workplace with a number of employees set out in column 1 of an
  item of Schedule I to this Part, the employer shall ensure that there
  is provided a first aid kit of the type set out in column 2 of that
  item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A first aid kit of type A, B, C or D shall contain
  the first aid supplies and equipment set out in column 1 of an item of
  Schedule II to this Part in the quantity, if any, set out in column 2
  of that item for that type.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A first aid kit of type A for use in a remote
  workplace shall, in addition to the contents required by subsection
  (2), contain the first aid supplies and equipment set out in column 1
  of an item of Schedule III to this Part in the quantity set out in
  column 2 of that item.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Prescription drugs or other medications not set out
  in Schedules II, III and IV to this Part shall not be stored in first
  aid kits or with first aid supplies.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
:::

- **[[16.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), if a
  hazard for skin or eye injury from a hazardous substance exists in the
  workplace, the employer shall ensure that shower facilities to wash
  the skin and eye wash facilities to irrigate the eyes are provided for
  immediate use by employees.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If it is not feasible to comply with subsection (1),
  the employer shall provide portable equipment that may be used in
  place of the facilities referred to in that subsection.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, due to adverse or extreme weather conditions, it
  is not feasible for the employer to comply with either subsection (1)
  or (2), the employer shall provide for all employees likely to be
  exposed to hazardous substances in the work place personal protection
  equipment for all parts of their bodies that may be exposed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-68, s. 14
- SOR/88-632, s. 72(F)
- SOR/94-263, ss. 60(F), 65(F)
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 43(F)
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 119](#1177651-1188210){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177651-1188210.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16.8-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 16.8"){.PITLink}
:::

### [First Aid Rooms]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895791 .Subheading}

- **[[16.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  200 or more employees are working at any time in a workplace, the
  employer shall ensure that a first aid room is provided and that it is
  clearly identified by a conspicuous sign.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A first aid room may be used for purposes other than
  first aid if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the minimum floor area required for first aid is
    maintained;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there is no potential for delay in the
    administration of first aid; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the use for those other purposes will not impede
    the treatment of an injured employee or pose a hazard to employees.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if a health unit or
  medical treatment facility at which medical treatment is provided
  without charge to employees is readily accessible.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 73(F)
- SOR/94-263, s. 61(F)
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
:::

- **[[16.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.10
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every first aid room provided in
  accordance with section 16.9 shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} under the supervision of a first aid attendant;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} located as close as possible to the work place and
    within easy access to a toilet room;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} situated on a minimum floor area of 10 m^2^ and
    constructed to allow for optimum ease of access to persons carrying
    a patient on a stretcher;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} maintained in an orderly and sanitary condition;
    and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} equipped with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a washbasin supplied with cold water and hot
      water that meets the standards set out in Part IX,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a storage cupboard and a counter,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a separate cubicle or curtained-off area with a
      cot or bed equipped with a moisture-protected mattress and two
      moisture-protected pillows,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a table and two or more chairs,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a telephone, or other effective means of
      communication, and an up-to-date list of appropriate emergency
      contacts and telephone numbers for use in emergencies, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the first aid supplies and equipment set out in
      Schedule IV to this Part.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In every first aid room referred to in subsection
  (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the air shall be changed at least once each hour;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the temperature

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} shall be maintained at not less than 21^o^C,
      measured one meter above the floor, when the outdoor temperature
      is 21^o^C or less, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if feasible, when the outdoor temperature in the
      shade exceeds 24^o^C, shall not exceed the outdoor temperature.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 74(F)
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 120(E)](#1177653-1188213){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177653-1188213.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16.10-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 16.10"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Transportation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895821 .Subheading}

**[[16.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.11 .sectionLabel}** Before assigning
employees to a workplace, the employer shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that for that workplace there is an ambulance
  service or other suitable means of transporting an injured or ill
  employee to a health unit or medical treatment facility; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide for that workplace a means of quickly
  summoning the ambulance service or other suitable means of
  transportation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
:::

### [First Aid Courses]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895827 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2019-246, s. 121

\]
:::

- **[[16.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The courses for the basic first aid
  certificate and for the standard first aid certificate shall include
  the subjects set out in Schedule V.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Basic, standard and advanced first aid attendant
  courses, including wilderness first aid courses, shall be given by a
  qualified person who holds a valid certification from an approved
  organization attesting that they are competent to deliver first aid
  courses.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For advanced first aid, including wilderness first
  aid, the employer shall determine, in consultation with the workplace
  committee or the health and safety representative, the first aid
  training requirements for the workplace, having regard to the
  particular nature of the workplace.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall review the training program with
  respect to advanced first aid, including wilderness first aid, in
  consultation with the workplace committee or the health and safety
  representative at least once every three years and whenever there is a
  change of circumstances that may affect the content of the training.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Basic, standard and advanced first aid certificates,
  including wilderness first aid courses, are valid for a maximum of
  three years from the date of issue.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/88-632, s. 75
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- SOR/2002-208, s. 35
- SOR/2012-271, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 122](#1177660-1188219){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177660-1188219.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16.12-20121207.html "Link to previous version of section 16.12"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-895836 .Subheading}

- **[[16.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If first aid is required by this
  Part to be rendered, the first aid attendant who renders the first aid
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} enter in a first aid record the following
    information:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date and time of the reporting of the injury
      or illness,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the full name of the injured or ill employee,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date, time and location of the occurrence
      of the injury or illness,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a brief description of the injury or illness,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a brief description of the first aid rendered, if
      any,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a brief description of arrangements made for the
      treatment or transportation of the injured or ill employee, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the names of witnesses, if applicable; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sign the first aid record beneath the information
    entered in accordance with paragraph (a).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information referred to in subsection (1) shall
  be entered

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if first aid was rendered to an employee at a
    remote workplace detached from the main party or on a snowmobile or
    other small vehicle, in the first aid record stored in the first aid
    kit at the site of the main party or work site; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, in the first aid record stored
    in the first aid kit.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall keep a first aid record containing
  information entered in accordance with subsection (2) for a period of
  two years beginning on the date of that entry.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Persons with access to first aid records shall keep
  the information contained in the records confidential, except as
  required for the purposes of meeting reporting obligations under Part
  XV.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} On receiving a written request from a workers'
  compensation authority for the province where the workplace is located
  or a medical practitioner, the employer shall provide an employee with
  a copy of the first aid record pertaining to the employee's treatment.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The employer shall keep a record of the expiry dates
  of the first aid certificates of the first aid attendants and make it
  available to them.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- SOR/2014-142, s. 10(E)
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 123](#1177662-1188221){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177662-1188221.html"}
:::

## [SCHEDULE V]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subsection 16.12(1))]{.OriginatingRef}[Subjects to Be Included in the Courses]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-895900 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** Basic first aid:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provision of basic first aid and the first aid
    attendant's role and obligations in relation to basic first aid;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} emergency scene management;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} cardiopulmonary resuscitation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} medical emergencies;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} shock and unconsciousness;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} anti-contamination procedures; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} wounds and bleeding.

- **2** Standard first aid:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provision of standard first aid and the first
    aid attendant's role and obligations in relation to standard first
    aid;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} emergency scene management;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} cardiopulmonary resuscitation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} medical emergencies;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} shock and unconsciousness;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} anti-contamination procedures;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} wounds and bleeding;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} fractures and their immobilization;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} chest injuries;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} head and spinal injuries;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} muscle, ligament and joint injuries;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} burns;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} eye injuries;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} pelvic, genital and abdominal injuries;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} movement and transportation of casualty;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} environmental illnesses and injuries;

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} toxicological emergencies; and

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} evacuation and transportation of casualties.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-328, s. 2
- SOR/2012-271, ss. 5 to 8
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 124(F)](#1177664-1188223){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177664-1188223.html"}
- [SOR/2019-246, s. 125(F)](#1177666-1188225){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1177666-1188225.html"}
:::
